/**
   * Downcasts this to a FunctionType, or returns null if this is not
   * a function.
   *
   * For the purposes of this function, we define a MaybeFunctionType as any
   * type in the sub-lattice
   * { x | LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE <= x <= GREATEST_FUNCTION_TYPE }
   * This definition excludes bottom types like NoType and NoObjectType.
   *
   * This definition is somewhat arbitrary and axiomatic, but this is the
   * definition that makes the most sense for the most callers.
   */

===== M0019 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionParamBuilder.java
Original range: 94-109
Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

390 |   private void validateVar(Node n) {
391 |     validateNodeType(Token.VAR, n);
392 |     this.validateMinimumChildCount(n, 1);
393 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
394 |       // Don't use the validateName here as the NAME is allowed to have
395 |       // a child.

243 |   private Node transformBlock(AstNode node) {
244 |     Node irNode = transform(node);
245 |     if (!irNode.isBlock()) {
246 |       if (irNode.isEmpty()) {
247 |         irNode.setType(Token.BLOCK);
248 |         irNode.setWasEmptyNode(true);

===== M0032 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3087-3115
Documentation:
/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
3087 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
3088 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
3089 |     if (info == null) {
3090 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
3091 |       if (parent == null) {
3092 |         return null;
3093 |       }
3094 | 
3095 |       if (parent.isName()) {
3096 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3097 |       } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3098 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3099 |       } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent)) {
3100 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3101 |       } else if (parent.isFunction()) {
3102 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3103 |       } else if (parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
3104 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3105 |       } else if ((parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3106 |                  parent.isOr() ||
3107 |                  parent.isAnd() ||
3108 |                  (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3109 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3110 |       } else if (parent.isCast()) {
3111 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3112 |       }
3113 |     }
3114 |     return info;
3115 |   }

===== M0033 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.position2charno(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 527-542
Documentation:

Method code:
527 |   private int position2charno(int position) {
528 |     int newlineIndex = Collections.binarySearch(newlines, position);
529 |     int lineIndex = -1;
530 |     if (newlineIndex >= 0) {
531 |       lineIndex = newlines.get(newlineIndex);
532 |     } else if (newlineIndex <= -2) {
533 |       lineIndex = newlines.get(-newlineIndex - 2);
534 |     }
535 | 
536 |     if (lineIndex == -1) {
537 |       return position;
538 |     } else {
539 |       // Subtract one for initial position being 0.
540 |       return position - lineIndex - 1;
541 |     }
542 |   }

===== M0039 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.hoistExterns(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1429-1465
Documentation:
/**
   * Hoists inputs with the @externs annotation into the externs list.
   */
Method code:
1429 |   private void hoistExterns(Node externsRoot) {
1430 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1431 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1432 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1433 |         // If we're doing scanning dependency info anyway, use that
1434 |         // information to skip sources that obviously aren't externs.
1435 |         if (!input.getProvides().isEmpty() || !input.getRequires().isEmpty()) {
1436 |           continue;
1437 |         }
1438 |       }
1439 | 
1440 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1441 | 
1442 |       // Inputs can have a null AST on a parse error.
1443 |       if (n == null) {
1444 |         continue;
1445 |       }
1446 | 
1447 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1448 |       if (info != null && info.isExterns()) {
1449 |         // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1450 |         // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1451 |         // the externs pile anyways.
1452 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1453 |         input.setIsExtern(true);
1454 | 
1455 |         input.getModule().remove(input);
1456 | 
1457 |         externs.add(input);
1458 |         staleInputs = true;
1459 |       }
1460 |     }
1461 | 
1462 |     if (staleInputs) {
1463 |       repartitionInputs();
1464 |     }
1465 |   }

===== M0044 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType,EquivalenceMethod)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 570-642
Documentation:
/**
   * An equivalence visitor.
   */
Method code:
570 |   boolean checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
571 |     if (this == that) {
572 |       return true;
573 |     }
574 | 
575 |     boolean thisUnknown = isUnknownType();
576 |     boolean thatUnknown = that.isUnknownType();
577 |     if (thisUnknown || thatUnknown) {
578 |       if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT) {
579 |         // If we're checking for invariance, the unknown type is invariant
580 |         // with everyone.
581 |         return true;
582 |       } else if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.DATA_FLOW) {
583 |         // If we're checking data flow, then two types are the same if they're
584 |         // both unknown.
585 |         return thisUnknown && thatUnknown;
586 |       } else if (thisUnknown && thatUnknown &&
587 |           (isNominalType() ^ that.isNominalType())) {
588 |         // If they're both unknown, but one is a nominal type and the other
589 |         // is not, then we should fail out immediately. This ensures that
590 |         // we won't unbox the unknowns further down.
591 |         return false;
592 |       }
593 |     }
594 | 
595 |     if (isUnionType() && that.isUnionType()) {
596 |       return toMaybeUnionType().checkUnionEquivalenceHelper(
597 |           that.toMaybeUnionType(), eqMethod);
598 |     }
599 | 
600 |     if (isFunctionType() && that.isFunctionType()) {
601 |       return toMaybeFunctionType().checkFunctionEquivalenceHelper(
602 |           that.toMaybeFunctionType(), eqMethod);
603 |     }
604 | 
605 |     if (isRecordType() && that.isRecordType()) {
606 |       return toMaybeRecordType().checkRecordEquivalenceHelper(
607 |           that.toMaybeRecordType(), eqMethod);
608 |     }
609 | 
610 |     if (!getTemplateTypeMap().checkEquivalenceHelper(
611 |         that.getTemplateTypeMap(), eqMethod)) {
612 |       return false;
613 |     }
614 | 
615 |     if (isNominalType() && that.isNominalType()) {
616 |       return toObjectType().getReferenceName().equals(
617 |           that.toObjectType().getReferenceName());
618 |     }
619 | 
620 |     if (isTemplateType() && that.isTemplateType()) {
621 |       return this == that;
622 |     }
623 | 
624 |     // Unbox other proxies.
625 |     if (this instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
626 |       return ((ProxyObjectType) this)
627 |           .getReferencedTypeInternal().checkEquivalenceHelper(
628 |               that, eqMethod);
629 |     }
630 | 
631 |     if (that instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
632 |       return checkEquivalenceHelper(
633 |           ((ProxyObjectType) that).getReferencedTypeInternal(),
634 |           eqMethod);
635 |     }
636 | 
637 |     // Relies on the fact that for the base {@link JSType}, only one
638 |     // instance of each sub-type will ever be created in a given registry, so
639 |     // there is no need to verify members. If the object pointers are not
640 |     // identical, then the type member must be different.
641 |     return this == that;
642 |   }

86 |   void addGuard(WarningsGuard guard) {

===== M0057 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 157-179
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
157 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
158 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.isCall());
159 | 
160 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
161 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
162 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
163 | 
164 |       // ignore cast nodes.
165 |       while (first.isCast()) {
166 |         first = first.getFirstChild();
167 |       }
168 | 
169 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
170 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
171 |       }
172 | 
173 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
174 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
175 |       if (first.isName() &&
176 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
177 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
178 |       }
179 |     }

791 |   private void validateMinimumChildCount(Node n, int i) {
792 |     boolean valid = false;
793 |     if (i == 1) {
794 |       valid = n.hasChildren();
795 |     } else if (i == 2) {
796 |       valid = n.hasMoreThanOneChild();
797 |     } else {
798 |       valid = n.getChildCount() >= i;
799 |     }
800 | 
801 |     if (!valid) {
802 |       violation(
803 |           "Expected at least " + i + " children, but was "
804 |               + n.getChildCount(), n);
805 |     }
806 |   }

===== M0078 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.BasicErrorManager.LeveledJSErrorComparator.compare(ErrorWithLevel,ErrorWithLevel)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/BasicErrorManager.java
Original range: 127-178
Documentation:

Method code:
127 |     @Override
128 |     public int compare(ErrorWithLevel p1, ErrorWithLevel p2) {
129 |       // null is the smallest value
130 |       if (p2 == null) {
131 |         if (p1 == null) {
132 |           return 0;
133 |         } else {
134 |           return P1_GT_P2;
135 |         }
136 |       }
137 | 
138 |       // check level
139 |       if (p1.level != p2.level) {
140 |         return p2.level.compareTo(p1.level);
141 |       }
142 | 
143 |       // sourceName comparison
144 |       String source1 = p1.error.sourceName;
145 |       String source2 = p2.error.sourceName;
146 |       if (source1 != null && source2 != null) {
147 |         int sourceCompare = source1.compareTo(source2);
148 |         if (sourceCompare != 0) {
149 |           return sourceCompare;
150 |         }
151 |       } else if (source1 == null && source2 != null) {
152 |         return P1_LT_P2;
153 |       } else if (source1 != null && source2 == null) {
154 |         return P1_GT_P2;
155 |       }
156 |       // lineno comparison
157 |       int lineno1 = p1.error.lineNumber;
158 |       int lineno2 = p2.error.lineNumber;
159 |       if (lineno1 != lineno2) {
160 |         return lineno1 - lineno2;
161 |       } else if (lineno1 < 0 && 0 <= lineno2) {
162 |         return P1_LT_P2;
163 |       } else if (0 <= lineno1 && lineno2 < 0) {
164 |         return P1_GT_P2;
165 |       }
166 |       // charno comparison
167 |       int charno1 = p1.error.getCharno();
168 |       int charno2 = p2.error.getCharno();
169 |       if (charno1 != charno2) {
170 |         return charno1 - charno2;
171 |       } else if (charno1 < 0 && 0 <= charno2) {
172 |         return P1_LT_P2;
173 |       } else if (0 <= charno1 && charno2 < 0) {
174 |         return P1_GT_P2;
175 |       }
176 |       // description
177 |       return p1.error.description.compareTo(p2.error.description);
178 |     }

===== M0082 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 399-424
Documentation:

Method code:
399 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
400 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
401 |     // two cases:
402 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
403 |     // and
404 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
405 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
406 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
407 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
408 |     //
409 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
410 |     //
411 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
412 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
413 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
414 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
415 |     //
416 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
417 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
418 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
419 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
420 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
421 |           registry, getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
422 |     }
423 |     setPrototype(baseType, propertyNode);
424 |   }

===== M0084 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 316-363
Documentation:

Method code:
316 |   @Override
317 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
318 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
319 |       return true;
320 |     }
321 | 
322 |     // Union types
323 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
324 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
325 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
326 |       return false;
327 |     }
328 | 
329 |     // record types
330 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
331 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
332 |     }
333 | 
334 |     // Interfaces
335 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
336 |     // to the interface instance.
337 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
338 |     FunctionType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
339 | 
340 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
341 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
342 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
343 |           return true;
344 |         }
345 |       }
346 |     } else if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
347 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
348 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
349 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
350 |           return true;
351 |         }
352 |       }
353 |     }
354 | 
355 |     // other prototype based objects
356 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
357 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
358 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
359 |       // to avoid guessing.
360 |       return true;
361 |     }
362 |     return thatObj != null && isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
363 |   }

===== M0098 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1232-1234
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
1232 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1233 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
1234 |   }

===== M0099 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createParameters(boolean,JSType...)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1278-1289
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1278 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1279 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1280 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1281 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1282 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1283 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1284 |       } else {
1285 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1286 |       }
1287 |     }
1288 |     return builder.build();
1289 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1974-1976
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1974 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1975 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1976 |   }

===== M0128 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeNoCheck(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 452-487
Documentation:
/** Set the prototype without doing any sanity checks. */
Method code:
452 |   private boolean setPrototypeNoCheck(ObjectType prototype, Node propertyNode) {
453 |     ObjectType oldPrototype = prototypeSlot == null
454 |         ? null : (ObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
455 |     boolean replacedPrototype = oldPrototype != null;
456 | 
457 |     this.prototypeSlot = new Property("prototype", prototype, true,
458 |         propertyNode == null ? source : propertyNode);
459 |     prototype.setOwnerFunction(this);
460 | 
461 |     if (oldPrototype != null) {
462 |       // Disassociating the old prototype makes this easier to debug--
463 |       // we don't have to worry about two prototypes running around.
464 |       oldPrototype.setOwnerFunction(null);
465 |     }
466 | 
467 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
468 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
469 |       if (superClass != null) {
470 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
471 |       }
472 | 
473 |       if (isInterface()) {
474 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
475 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
476 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
477 |           }
478 |         }
479 |       }
480 |     }
481 | 
482 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
483 |       clearCachedValues();
484 |     }
485 | 
486 |     return true;
487 |   }

===== M0141 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 544-577
Documentation:
/** Traverses a function. */
Method code:
544 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
545 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
546 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
547 | 
548 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
549 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
550 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
551 | 
552 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
553 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
554 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
555 |     }
556 | 
557 |     curNode = n;
558 |     pushScope(n);
559 | 
560 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
561 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
562 |       // scope.
563 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
564 |     }
565 | 
566 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
567 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
568 | 
569 |     // Args
570 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
571 | 
572 |     // Body
573 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null && body.isBlock(), body);
574 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
575 | 
576 |     popScope();
577 |   }

===== M0167 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(StaticSourceFile,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 107-147
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
107 |   public static ParseResult parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
108 |                                   String sourceString,
109 |                                   Config config,
110 |                                   ErrorReporter errorReporter,
111 |                                   Logger logger) throws IOException {
112 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
113 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
114 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
115 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
116 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
117 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
118 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
119 | 
120 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
121 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
122 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
123 | 
124 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
125 | 
126 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
127 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
128 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
129 | 
130 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
131 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
132 |     try {
133 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
134 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
135 |       logger.info(
136 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
137 |     } finally {
138 |       Context.exit();
139 |     }
140 |     Node root = null;
141 |     if (astRoot != null) {
142 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
143 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
144 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
145 |     }
146 |     return new ParseResult(root, astRoot);
147 |   }

===== M0182 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getFunctionName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 426-445
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the function's name. This method recognizes five forms:
   * <ul>
   * <li>{@code function name() ...}</li>
   * <li>{@code var name = function() ...}</li>
   * <li>{@code qualified.name = function() ...}</li>
   * <li>{@code var name2 = function name1() ...}</li>
   * <li>{@code qualified.name2 = function name1() ...}</li>
   * </ul>
   * In two last cases with named function expressions, the second name is
   * returned (the variable of qualified name).
   *
   * @param n a node whose type is {@link Token#FUNCTION}
   * @return the function's name, or {@code null} if it has no name
   */
Method code:
426 |   static String getFunctionName(Node n) {
427 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
428 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
429 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
430 |       case Token.NAME:
431 |         // var name = function() ...
432 |         // var name2 = function name1() ...
433 |         return parent.getQualifiedName();
434 | 
435 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
436 |         // qualified.name = function() ...
437 |         // qualified.name2 = function name1() ...
438 |         return parent.getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
439 | 
440 |       default:
441 |         // function name() ...
442 |         String name = n.getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
443 |         return name;
444 |     }
445 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transformNodeWithInlineJsDoc(AstNode)

Documentation:
/**
   * NAMEs in parameters or variable declarations are special, because they can
   * have inline type docs attached.
   *
   * function f(/** string &#42;/ x) {}
   * annotates 'x' as a string.
   *
   * @see <a href="http://code.google.com/p/jsdoc-toolkit/wiki/InlineDocs">
   *   Using Inline Doc Comments</a>
   */
Method code:
408 |   private Node transformNodeWithInlineJsDoc(AstNode node) {
409 |     Node irNode = justTransform(node);
410 |     Comment comment = node.getJsDocNode();
411 |     if (comment != null) {
412 |       JSDocInfo info = parseInlineTypeDoc(comment, irNode);
413 |       if (info != null) {
414 |         irNode.setJSDocInfo(info);
415 |       }
416 |     }
417 |     setSourceInfo(irNode, node);
418 |     return irNode;
419 |   }

===== M0201 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 505-521
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
505 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
506 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
507 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
508 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
509 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
510 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
511 |         String inputName = input.getName();
512 | 
513 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
514 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
515 |         // will get an error down the line.
516 |         inputs.add(input);
517 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
518 |       }
519 |     }
520 |     return inputs;
521 |   }

168 |   private int getTemplateTypeIndex(TemplateType key) {
169 |     int maxIndex = Math.min(templateKeys.size(), templateValues.size());
170 |     for (int i = maxIndex - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
171 |       if (templateKeys.get(i) == key) {
172 |         return i;
173 |       }
174 |     }
175 |     return -1;
176 |   }

Original range: 198-221
Documentation:

Method code:
198 |   public static Node transformTree(AstRoot node,
199 |                                    StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
200 |                                    String sourceString,
201 |                                    Config config,
202 |                                    ErrorReporter errorReporter) {
203 |     IRFactory irFactory = new IRFactory(sourceString, sourceFile,
204 |         config, errorReporter);
205 |     Node irNode = irFactory.transform(node);
206 | 
207 |     if (node.getComments() != null) {
208 |       for (Comment comment : node.getComments()) {
209 |         if (comment.getCommentType() == CommentType.JSDOC &&
210 |             !irFactory.parsedComments.contains(comment)) {
211 |           irFactory.handlePossibleFileOverviewJsDoc(comment, irNode);
212 |         } else if (comment.getCommentType() == CommentType.BLOCK_COMMENT) {
213 |           irFactory.handleBlockComment(comment);
214 |         }
215 |       }
216 |     }
217 | 
218 |     irFactory.setFileOverviewJsDoc(irNode);
219 | 
220 |     return irNode;
221 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * For each message such as "Not a good use of {0}", replace the place
   * holder {0} with a wild card that matches all possible strings.
   * Also put the any non-place-holder in quotes for regex matching later.
   */
Method code:
80 |   private Pattern replacePlaceHolders(String s) {
81 |     s = Pattern.quote(s);
82 |     return Pattern.compile(s.replaceAll("\\{\\d+\\}", "\\\\E.*\\\\Q"));
83 |   }

/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totaling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
236 |     this.type = type;
237 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
269 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
271 |                     + "try/finally block. "
272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

===== M0284 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 785-868
Documentation:

Method code:
785 |     @Override
786 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
787 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
788 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
789 |       if (name == null) {
790 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
791 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
792 |           errorReporter.error(
793 |             "unnamed function statement",
794 |             sourceName,
795 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
796 | 
797 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
798 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
799 |         }
800 |         name = new Name();
801 |         name.setIdentifier("");
802 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
803 |       }
804 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
805 |       // if the function has an inline return annotation, attach it
806 |       Node newName = transformNodeWithInlineJsDoc(name);
807 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
808 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
809 |         // declaration.
810 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
811 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
812 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
813 |         // same line as the function keyword.
814 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
815 |             functionNode.getLp();
816 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
817 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
818 |       }
819 | 
820 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
821 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
822 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
823 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
824 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
825 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
826 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
827 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
828 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
829 |       if (fnName != null) {
830 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
831 |       } else {
832 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
833 |       }
834 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
835 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
836 | 
837 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
838 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
839 |         Node paramNode = transformNodeWithInlineJsDoc(param);
840 |         // When in ideMode Rhino can generate a param list with only a single
841 |         // ErrorNode. This is transformed into an EMPTY node. Drop this node in
842 |         // ideMode to keep the AST in a valid state.
843 |         if (paramNode.isName()) {
844 |           lp.addChildToBack(paramNode);
845 |         } else {
846 |           // We expect this in ideMode or when there is an error handling
847 |           // destructuring parameter assignments which aren't supported
848 |           // (an error has already been reported).
849 |           Preconditions.checkState(
850 |               config.isIdeMode
851 |               || paramNode.isObjectLit()
852 |               || paramNode.isArrayLit());
853 |         }
854 |       }
855 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
856 | 
857 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
858 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
859 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
860 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
861 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
862 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
863 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
864 |       }
865 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
866 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
867 |      return node;
868 |     }

===== M0285 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1300-1424
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1300 |   Node parseInputs() {
1301 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1302 | 
1303 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1304 |     // individual file parse trees.
1305 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1306 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1307 |     }
1308 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1309 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1310 |     }
1311 | 
1312 |     // Parse main JS sources.
1313 |     jsRoot = IR.block();
1314 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1315 | 
1316 |     externsRoot = IR.block();
1317 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1318 | 
1319 |     externAndJsRoot = IR.block(externsRoot, jsRoot);
1320 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1321 | 
1322 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1323 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot, options.tracer);
1324 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1325 |     }
1326 | 
1327 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer(PARSING_PASS_NAME);
1328 | 
1329 |     try {
1330 |       // Parse externs sources.
1331 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1332 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1333 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1334 |           return null;
1335 |         }
1336 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1337 |       }
1338 | 
1339 |       // Modules inferred in ProcessCommonJS pass.
1340 |       if (options.transformAMDToCJSModules || options.processCommonJSModules) {
1341 |         processAMDAndCommonJSModules();
1342 |       }
1343 | 
1344 |       hoistExterns(externsRoot);
1345 | 
1346 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1347 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1348 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1349 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1350 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1351 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1352 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1353 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1354 |           }
1355 |         }
1356 | 
1357 |         try {
1358 |           inputs =
1359 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1360 |               .manageDependencies(options.dependencyOptions, inputs);
1361 |           staleInputs = true;
1362 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1363 |           report(JSError.make(
1364 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1365 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1366 |           report(JSError.make(
1367 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1368 |         } catch (JSModuleGraph.MissingModuleException e) {
1369 |           report(JSError.make(
1370 |               MISSING_MODULE_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1371 |         }
1372 | 
1373 |         // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1374 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1375 |           return null;
1376 |         }
1377 |       }
1378 | 
1379 |       hoistNoCompileFiles();
1380 | 
1381 |       if (staleInputs) {
1382 |         repartitionInputs();
1383 |       }
1384 | 
1385 |       // Build the AST.
1386 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1387 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1388 |         if (n == null) {
1389 |           continue;
1390 |         }
1391 | 
1392 |         if (devMode) {
1393 |           runSanityCheck();
1394 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1395 |             return null;
1396 |           }
1397 |         }
1398 | 
1399 |         // TODO(johnlenz): we shouldn't need to check both isExternExportsEnabled and
1400 |         // externExportsPath.
1401 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1402 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null ||
1403 |             options.isExternExportsEnabled() ||
1404 |             options.externExportsPath != null) {
1405 | 
1406 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1407 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1408 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1409 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1410 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1411 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1412 |         }
1413 | 
1414 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1415 |       }
1416 | 
1417 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1418 |         return null;
1419 |       }
1420 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1421 |     } finally {
1422 |       stopTracer(tracer, PARSING_PASS_NAME);
1423 |     }
1424 |   }

===== M0298 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ScopedAliases.Traversal.findAliases(NodeTraversal)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ScopedAliases.java
Original range: 352-422
Documentation:

Method code:
352 |     private void findAliases(NodeTraversal t) {
353 |       Scope scope = t.getScope();
354 |       for (Var v : scope.getVarIterable()) {
355 |         Node n = v.getNode();
356 |         Node parent = n.getParent();
357 |         boolean isVar = parent.isVar();
358 |         if (isVar && n.getFirstChild() != null && n.getFirstChild().isQualifiedName()) {
359 |           recordAlias(v);
360 |         } else if (v.isBleedingFunction()) {
361 |           // Bleeding functions already get a BAD_PARAMETERS error, so just
362 |           // do nothing.
363 |         } else if (parent.getType() == Token.LP) {
364 |           // Parameters of the scope function also get a BAD_PARAMETERS
365 |           // error.
366 |         } else if (isVar) {
367 |           Node grandparent = parent.getParent();
368 |           Node value = n.hasChildren() ?
369 |               v.getInitialValue().detachFromParent() :
370 |               null;
371 |           Node varNode = parent;
372 | 
373 |           String name = n.getString();
374 |           int nameCount = scopedAliasNames.count(name);
375 |           scopedAliasNames.add(name);
376 |           String globalName =
377 |               "$jscomp.scope." + name + (nameCount == 0 ? "" : ("$" + nameCount));
378 | 
379 |           compiler.ensureLibraryInjected("base");
380 | 
381 |           // First, we need to free up the function expression (EXPR)
382 |           // to be used in another expression.
383 |             // Replace "function NAME() { ... }" with "var NAME;".
384 | 
385 |             // We can't keep the local name on the function expression,
386 |             // because IE is buggy and will leak the name into the global
387 |             // scope. This is covered in more detail here:
388 |             // http://wiki.ecmascript.org/lib/exe/fetch.php?id=resources:resources&cache=cache&media=resources:jscriptdeviationsfromes3.pdf
389 |             //
390 |             // This will only cause problems if this is a hoisted, recursive
391 |             // function, and the programmer is using the hoisting.
392 | 
393 |               // If this is a VAR, we can just detach the expression and
394 |               // the tree will still be valid.
395 | 
396 |           // Add $jscomp.scope.name = EXPR;
397 |           // Make sure we copy over all the jsdoc and debug info.
398 |           if (value != null || v.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
399 |             Node newDecl = NodeUtil.newQualifiedNameNodeDeclaration(
400 |                 compiler.getCodingConvention(),
401 |                 globalName,
402 |                 value,
403 |                 v.getJSDocInfo())
404 |                 .useSourceInfoIfMissingFromForTree(n);
405 |             NodeUtil.setDebugInformation(
406 |                 newDecl.getFirstChild().getFirstChild(), n, name);
407 | 
408 |               grandparent.addChildBefore(newDecl, varNode);
409 |           }
410 | 
411 |           // Rewrite "var name = EXPR;" to "var name = $jscomp.scope.name;"
412 |           v.getNameNode().addChildToFront(
413 |               NodeUtil.newQualifiedNameNode(
414 |                   compiler.getCodingConvention(), globalName, n, name));
415 | 
416 |           recordAlias(v);
417 |         } else {
418 |           // Do not other kinds of local symbols, like catch params.
419 |           report(t, n, GOOG_SCOPE_NON_ALIAS_LOCAL, n.getString());
420 |         }
421 |       }
422 |     }

===== M0301 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1515-1566
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recur Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @param shallow If true, the method doesn't recur into inner functions.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1515 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(
1516 |       Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recur, boolean shallow) {
1517 |     if (type != node.getType()
1518 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1519 |         || this.getClass() != node.getClass()) {
1520 |       return false;
1521 |     }
1522 | 
1523 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1524 |       return false;
1525 |     }
1526 | 
1527 |     if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1528 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1529 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1530 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1531 |         return false;
1532 |       }
1533 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING || type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1534 |       if (type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1535 |         int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1536 |         int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1537 |         if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1538 |           return false;
1539 |         }
1540 |       }
1541 | 
1542 |       int slashV1 = this.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1543 |       int slashV2 = node.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1544 |       if (slashV1 != slashV2) {
1545 |         return false;
1546 |       }
1547 |     } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
1548 |       if (this.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL) != node.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL)) {
1549 |         return false;
1550 |       }
1551 |     }
1552 | 
1553 |     if (recur) {
1554 |       Node n, n2;
1555 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1556 |            n != null;
1557 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1558 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(
1559 |             n2, compareJsType, !(shallow && n.isFunction()), shallow)) {
1560 |           return false;
1561 |         }
1562 |       }
1563 |     }
1564 | 
1565 |     return true;
1566 |   }

===== M0306 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 120-281
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
120 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
121 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
122 |     // just bail out early here.
123 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
124 |       return this;
125 |     }
126 | 
127 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
128 | 
129 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
130 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
131 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
132 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
133 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
134 |     }
135 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
136 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
137 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
138 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
139 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
140 |         }
141 |       } else {
142 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
143 |           return this;
144 |         }
145 | 
146 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
147 |         // own bizarre sub-lattice. See the comments on
148 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
149 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
150 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
151 |           FunctionType other =
152 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
153 |           FunctionType supremum =
154 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
155 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
156 |           result = null;
157 |           return this;
158 |         }
159 | 
160 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
161 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
162 |         // one another.
163 |         int currentIndex = 0;
164 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
165 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
166 |           boolean removeCurrent = false;
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType() ||
176 |               alternate.hasAnyTemplateTypes() ||
177 |               current.hasAnyTemplateTypes()) {
178 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
179 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
180 |               return this;
181 |             }
182 |           } else {
183 | 
184 |             // Because "Foo" and "Foo.<?>" are roughly equivalent
185 |             // templatized types, special care is needed when building the
186 |             // union. For example:
187 |             //   Object is consider a subtype of Object.<string>
188 |             // but we want to leave "Object" not "Object.<string>" when
189 |             // building the subtype.
190 |             //
191 | 
192 |             if (alternate.isTemplatizedType() || current.isTemplatizedType()) {
193 |               // Cases:
194 |               // 1) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Object ==> Object
195 |               // 2) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Array ==> Array
196 |               // 3) alternate:Object.<string> and
197 |               //    current:Array ==> Array|Object.<string>
198 |               // 4) alternate:Object and current:Array.<string> ==> Object
199 |               // 5) alternate:Array and current:Array.<string> ==> Array
200 |               // 6) alternate:Array and
201 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Array|Object.<string>
202 |               // 7) alternate:Array.<string> and
203 |               //    current:Array.<number> ==> Array.<?>
204 |               // 8) alternate:Array.<string> and
205 |               //    current:Array.<string> ==> Array.<string>
206 |               // 9) alternate:Array.<string> and
207 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Object.<string>|Array.<string>
208 | 
209 |               if (!current.isTemplatizedType()) {
210 |                 if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
211 |                   // case 1, 2
212 |                   return this;
213 |                 }
214 |                 // case 3: leave current, add alternate
215 |               } else if (!alternate.isTemplatizedType()) {
216 |                 if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
217 |                   // case 4, 5
218 |                   removeCurrent = true;
219 |                 }
220 |                 // case 6: leave current, add alternate
221 |               } else {
222 |                 Preconditions.checkState(current.isTemplatizedType()
223 |                     && alternate.isTemplatizedType());
224 |                 TemplatizedType templatizedAlternate = alternate.toMaybeTemplatizedType();
225 |                 TemplatizedType templatizedCurrent = current.toMaybeTemplatizedType();
226 | 
227 |                 if (templatizedCurrent.wrapsSameRawType(templatizedAlternate)) {
228 |                   if (alternate.getTemplateTypeMap().checkEquivalenceHelper(
229 |                       current.getTemplateTypeMap(),
230 |                       EquivalenceMethod.IDENTITY)) {
231 |                     // case 8
232 |                     return this;
233 |                   } else {
234 |                     // TODO(johnlenz): should we leave both types?
235 |                     // case 7: add a merged alternate
236 |                     // We currently merge to the templatized types to "unknown"
237 |                     // which is equivalent to the raw type.
238 |                     JSType merged = templatizedCurrent
239 |                         .getReferencedObjTypeInternal();
240 |                     return addAlternate(merged);
241 |                   }
242 |                 }
243 |                 // case 9: leave current, add alternate
244 |               }
245 |               // Otherwise leave both templatized types.
246 |             } else if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
247 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
248 |               return this;
249 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
250 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
251 |               removeCurrent = true;
252 |             }
253 |           }
254 | 
255 |           if (removeCurrent) {
256 |             it.remove();
257 | 
258 |             if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
259 |               functionTypePosition = -1;
260 |             } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
261 |               functionTypePosition--;
262 |               currentIndex--;
263 |             }
264 |           }
265 |           currentIndex++;
266 |         }
267 | 
268 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
269 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
270 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
271 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
272 |         }
273 | 
274 |         alternates.add(alternate);
275 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
276 |       }
277 |     } else {
278 |       result = null;
279 |     }
280 |     return this;
281 |   }

===== M0308 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SuppressDocWarningsGuard.level(JSError)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SuppressDocWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 51-101
Documentation:

Method code:
51 |   @Override
52 |   public CheckLevel level(JSError error) {
53 |     Node node = error.node;
54 |     if (node != null) {
55 |       boolean visitedFunction = false;
56 |       for (Node current = node;
57 |            current != null;
58 |            current = current.getParent()) {
59 |         int type = current.getType();
60 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
61 | 
62 |         if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
63 |           info = NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(current);
64 |           visitedFunction = true;
65 |         } else if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
66 |           info = current.getJSDocInfo();
67 |         } else if (current.isVar() || current.isAssign()) {
68 |           // There's one edge case we're worried about:
69 |           // if the warning points to an assigment to a function, we
70 |           // want the suppressions on that function to apply.
71 |           // It's OK if we double-count some cases.
72 |           Node rhs = NodeUtil.getRValueOfLValue(current.getFirstChild());
73 |           if (rhs != null) {
74 |             if (rhs.isCast()) {
75 |               rhs = rhs.getFirstChild();
76 |             }
77 | 
78 |             if (rhs.isFunction() && !visitedFunction) {
79 |               info = NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(current);
80 |             }
81 |           }
82 |         }
83 | 
84 |         if (info != null) {
85 |           for (String suppressor : info.getSuppressions()) {
86 |             WarningsGuard guard = suppressors.get(suppressor);
87 | 
88 |             // Some @suppress tags are for other tools, and
89 |             // may not have a warnings guard.
90 |             if (guard != null) {
91 |               CheckLevel newLevel = guard.level(error);
92 |               if (newLevel != null) {
93 |                 return newLevel;
94 |               }
95 |             }
96 |           }
97 |         }
98 |       }

===== M0329 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 567-580
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
567 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
568 |       Node ret;
569 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
570 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name) n);
571 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
572 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral) n);
573 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
574 |       } else {
575 |         ret = transform(n);
576 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
577 |       }
578 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.isString());
579 |       return ret;
580 |     }

===== M0340 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JsAst.parse(AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JsAst.java
Original range: 86-115
Documentation:

Method code:
86 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler) {
87 |     int startErrorCount = compiler.getErrorManager().getErrorCount();
88 |     try {
89 |       ParserRunner.ParseResult result = ParserRunner.parse(sourceFile, sourceFile.getCode(),
90 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
91 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
92 |           logger_);
93 |       root = result.ast;
94 |       compiler.setOldParseTree(sourceFile.getName(), result.oldAst);
95 |     } catch (IOException e) {
96 |       compiler.report(
97 |           JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceFile.getName()));
98 |     }
99 | 
100 | 
101 |     if (root == null ||
102 |         // Most passes try to report as many errors as possible,
103 |         // so there may already be errors. We only care if there were
104 |         // errors in the code we just parsed.
105 |         (compiler.getErrorManager().getErrorCount() > startErrorCount && !compiler.isIdeMode())) {
106 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
107 |       root = IR.script();
108 |     } else {
109 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
110 |     }
111 | 
112 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
113 |     // the source file and module.
114 |     root.setStaticSourceFile(sourceFile);
115 |   }

===== M0342 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1194-1202
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1194 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1195 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1196 |       return -1;
1197 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1198 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1199 |     } else {
1200 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1201 |     }
1202 |   }

===== M0346 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 529-541
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
529 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
530 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
531 |          current != null;
532 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
533 |       if (current.isTemplatizedType()) {
534 |         current = current.toMaybeTemplatizedType().getReferencedType();
535 |       }
536 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
537 |         return true;
538 |       }
539 |     }
540 |     return false;
541 |   }

===== M0368 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1240-1300
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1240 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1241 |     // unknown
1242 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1243 |       return true;
1244 |     }
1245 |     // all type
1246 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1247 |       return true;
1248 |     }
1249 |     // equality
1250 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1251 |       return true;
1252 |     }
1253 |     // unions
1254 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1255 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1256 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1257 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1258 |           return true;
1259 |         }
1260 |       }
1261 |       return false;
1262 |     }
1263 | 
1264 |     // TemplateTypeMaps. This check only returns false if the TemplateTypeMaps
1265 |     // are not equivalent.
1266 |     TemplateTypeMap thisTypeParams = thisType.getTemplateTypeMap();
1267 |     TemplateTypeMap thatTypeParams = thatType.getTemplateTypeMap();
1268 |     boolean templateMatch = true;
1269 |     if (isExemptFromTemplateTypeInvariance(thatType)) {
1270 |       // Array and Object are exempt from template type invariance; their
1271 |       // template types maps are considered a match only if the ObjectElementKey
1272 |       // values are subtypes/supertypes of one another.
1273 |       TemplateType key = thisType.registry.getObjectElementKey();
1274 |       JSType thisElement = thisTypeParams.getTemplateType(key);
1275 |       JSType thatElement = thatTypeParams.getTemplateType(key);
1276 | 
1277 |       templateMatch = thisElement.isSubtype(thatElement)
1278 |           || thatElement.isSubtype(thisElement);
1279 |     } else {
1280 |       templateMatch = thisTypeParams.checkEquivalenceHelper(
1281 |           thatTypeParams, EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT);
1282 |     }
1283 |     if (!templateMatch) {
1284 |       return false;
1285 |     }
1286 | 
1287 |     // Templatized types. The above check guarantees TemplateTypeMap
1288 |     // equivalence; check if the base type is a subtype.
1289 |     if (thisType.isTemplatizedType()) {
1290 |       return thisType.toMaybeTemplatizedType().getReferencedType().isSubtype(
1291 |               thatType);
1292 |     }
1293 | 
1294 |     // proxy types
1295 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1296 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1297 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1298 |     }
1299 |     return false;
1300 |   }

===== M0374 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getQualifiedName()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1576-1591
Documentation:
/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1576 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1577 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1578 |       String name = getString();
1579 |       return name.isEmpty() ? null : name;
1580 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1581 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1582 |       if (left == null) {
1583 |         return null;
1584 |       }
1585 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1586 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1587 |       return "this";
1588 |     } else {
1589 |       return null;
1590 |     }
1591 |   }

===== M0375 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.reconcileOptionsWithGuards()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 313-352
Documentation:
/**
   * When the CompilerOptions and its WarningsGuard overlap, reconcile
   * any discrepencies.
   */
Method code:
313 |   protected void reconcileOptionsWithGuards() {
314 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
315 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
316 |       options.checkTypes = true;
317 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
318 |       options.checkTypes = false;
319 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
320 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
321 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
322 |       // parser type warnings.
323 |       options.setWarningLevel(
324 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
325 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
326 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
327 |     }
328 | 
329 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
330 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
331 |       options.setWarningLevel(
332 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
333 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
334 |     }
335 | 
336 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
337 |       options.setWarningLevel(
338 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
339 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
340 |     }
341 | 
342 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
343 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
344 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
345 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
346 |     // error off.
347 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
348 |         !options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
349 |       options.setWarningLevel(
350 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF);
351 |     }
352 |   }

===== M0379 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 515-541
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
515 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
516 |     int type = n.getType();
517 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
518 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
519 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
520 |     }
521 | 
522 |     curNode = n;
523 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) {
524 |       return;
525 |     }
526 | 
527 |     if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
528 |       traverseFunction(n, parent);
529 |     } else {
530 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
531 |         // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
532 |         // would no longer point to the true next
533 |         Node next = child.getNext();
534 |         traverseBranch(child, n);
535 |         child = next;
536 |       }
537 |     }
538 | 
539 |     curNode = n;
540 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
541 |   }

===== M0380 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 797-810
Documentation:
/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
797 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
798 |     if (item == null) {
799 |       return null;
800 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
801 |       return item.getNext();
802 |     } else {
803 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
804 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
805 |         return item.chain(result);
806 |       } else {
807 |         return item;
808 |       }
809 |     }
810 |   }

===== M0385 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 112-166
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
112 |   private void scanVars(Node n) {
113 |     switch (n.getType()) {
114 |       case Token.VAR:
115 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
116 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
117 |              child != null;) {
118 |           Node next = child.getNext();
119 |           declareVar(child);
120 |           child = next;
121 |         }
122 |         return;
123 | 
124 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
125 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
126 |           return;
127 |         }
128 | 
129 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
130 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
131 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
132 |           return;
133 |         }
134 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
135 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
136 | 
137 |       case Token.CATCH:
138 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
139 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().isName());
140 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
141 |         // is the code block
142 | 
143 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
144 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
145 | 
146 |         declareVar(var);
147 |         scanVars(block);
148 |         return;  // only one child to scan
149 | 
150 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
151 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
152 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
153 |         break;
154 |     }
155 | 
156 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
157 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
158 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
159 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
160 |            child != null;) {
161 |         Node next = child.getNext();
162 |         scanVars(child);
163 |         child = next;
164 |       }
165 |     }
166 |   }

===== M0400 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ScopedAliases.Traversal.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ScopedAliases.java
Original range: 499-564
Documentation:

Method code:
499 |     @Override
500 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
501 |       if (isCallToScopeMethod(n)) {
502 |         validateScopeCall(t, n, n.getParent());
503 |       }
504 | 
505 |       if (t.getScopeDepth() < 2) {
506 |         return;
507 |       }
508 | 
509 |       int type = n.getType();
510 |       Var aliasVar = null;
511 |       if (type == Token.NAME) {
512 |         String name = n.getString();
513 |         Var lexicalVar = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
514 |         if (lexicalVar != null && lexicalVar == aliases.get(name)) {
515 |           aliasVar = lexicalVar;
516 |         }
517 |       }
518 | 
519 |       // Validate the top-level of the goog.scope block.
520 |       if (t.getScopeDepth() == 2) {
521 |         if (aliasVar != null && NodeUtil.isLValue(n)) {
522 |           if (aliasVar.getNode() == n) {
523 |             aliasDefinitionsInOrder.add(n);
524 | 
525 |             // Return early, to ensure that we don't record a definition
526 |             // twice.
527 |             return;
528 |           } else {
529 |             report(t, n, GOOG_SCOPE_ALIAS_REDEFINED, n.getString());
530 |           }
531 |         }
532 | 
533 |         if (type == Token.RETURN) {
534 |           report(t, n, GOOG_SCOPE_USES_RETURN);
535 |         } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
536 |           report(t, n, GOOG_SCOPE_REFERENCES_THIS);
537 |         } else if (type == Token.THROW) {
538 |           report(t, n, GOOG_SCOPE_USES_THROW);
539 |         }
540 |       }
541 | 
542 |       // Validate all descendent scopes of the goog.scope block.
543 |       if (t.getScopeDepth() >= 2) {
544 |         // Check if this name points to an alias.
545 |         if (aliasVar != null) {
546 |           // Note, to support the transitive case, it's important we don't
547 |           // clone aliasedNode here.  For example,
548 |           // var g = goog; var d = g.dom; d.createElement('DIV');
549 |           // The node in aliasedNode (which is "g") will be replaced in the
550 |           // changes pass above with "goog".  If we cloned here, we'd end up
551 |           // with <code>g.dom.createElement('DIV')</code>.
552 |           aliasUsages.add(new AliasedNode(aliasVar, n));
553 |         }
554 | 
555 |         JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
556 |         if (info != null) {
557 |           for (Node node : info.getTypeNodes()) {
558 |             fixTypeNode(node);
559 |           }
560 |         }
561 | 
562 |         // TODO(robbyw): Error for goog.scope not at root.
563 |       }
564 |     }

===== M0410 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)

Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
630 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
631 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
632 |     if (typeSet == null) {
633 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
634 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
635 |     }
636 | 
637 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
638 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
639 | 
640 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
641 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
642 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 628-643
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * ECMA-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
628 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
629 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
630 |       Set<String> directives = null;
631 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
632 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
633 |         if (directives == null) {
634 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
635 |         } else {
636 |           directives.add(directive);
637 |         }
638 |       }
639 | 
640 |       if (directives != null) {
641 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
642 |       }
643 |     }

171 |     this.templateNode = createTemplateNode();
172 | 
173 |     switch (config.languageMode) {
174 |       case ECMASCRIPT3:
175 |         reservedKeywords = null; // use TokenStream.isKeyword instead
176 |         break;
177 |       case ECMASCRIPT5:
178 |         reservedKeywords = ES5_RESERVED_KEYWORDS;
179 |         break;
180 |       case ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT:
181 |         reservedKeywords = ES5_STRICT_RESERVED_KEYWORDS;
182 |         break;
183 |       default:
184 |         throw new IllegalStateException("unknown language mode");
185 |     }
186 |   }

Original range: 120-285
